602 |     private void defineObjectLiteral(Node objectLit) {
603 |       // Handle the @lends annotation.
604 |       JSType type = null;
605 |       JSDocInfo info = objectLit.getJSDocInfo();
606 |       if (info != null &&
607 |           info.getLendsName() != null) {
608 |         String lendsName = info.getLendsName();
609 |         Var lendsVar = scope.getVar(lendsName);
610 |         if (lendsVar == null) {
611 |           compiler.report(
612 |               JSError.make(sourceName, objectLit, UNKNOWN_LENDS, lendsName));
613 |         } else {
614 |           type = lendsVar.getType();
615 |           if (type == null) {
616 |             type = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
617 |           }
618 |           if (!type.isSubtype(typeRegistry.getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
619 |             compiler.report(
620 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, objectLit, LENDS_ON_NON_OBJECT,
621 |                     lendsName, type.toString()));
622 |             type = null;
623 |           } else {
624 |             objectLit.setJSType(type);
625 |           }
626 |         }
627 |       }
628 | 
629 |       info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(objectLit);
630 |       Node lValue = NodeUtil.getBestLValue(objectLit);
631 |       String lValueName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(lValue);
632 |       boolean createdEnumType = false;
633 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
634 |         type = createEnumTypeFromNodes(objectLit, lValueName, info, lValue);
635 |         createdEnumType = true;
636 |       }
637 | 
638 |       if (type == null) {
639 |         type = typeRegistry.createAnonymousObjectType();
640 |       }
641 | 
642 |       setDeferredType(objectLit, type);
643 | 
644 |       // If this is an enum, the properties were already taken care of above.
645 |       processObjectLitProperties(
646 |           objectLit, ObjectType.cast(objectLit.getJSType()), !createdEnumType);
647 |     }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 242-305
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
242 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
243 |     this.options = options;
244 |     if (errorManager == null) {
245 |       if (outStream == null) {
246 |         setErrorManager(
247 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
248 |       } else {
249 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
250 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
251 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
252 |         setErrorManager(printer);
253 |       }
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
257 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
258 |       options.checkTypes = true;
259 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
260 |       options.checkTypes = false;
261 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
262 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
263 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
264 |       // parser type warnings.
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
267 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
268 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
272 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
273 |       options.setWarningLevel(
274 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
275 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
279 |       options.setWarningLevel(
280 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
281 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
282 |     }
283 | 
284 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
285 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
286 |     guards.add(
287 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
288 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
289 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
290 | 
291 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
292 | 
293 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
294 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
295 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
296 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
297 |     // error off.
298 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
299 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
300 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
301 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
305 |   }

/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param associatedNode The source node containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns
   *     {@code null}
   */
Method code:
124 |   public JSDocInfo build(Node associatedNode) {
125 |     if (populated) {
126 |       JSDocInfo built = currentInfo;
127 |       built.setAssociatedNode(associatedNode);
128 |       populateDefaults(built);
129 |       populated = false;
130 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
131 |       return built;
132 |     } else {
133 |       return null;
134 |     }
135 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

/**
   * Creates a record type.
   *
   * @param registry The type registry under which this type lives.
   * @param properties A map of all the properties of this record type.
   * @throws IllegalStateException if the {@code RecordProperty} associated
   *         with a property is null.

Method code:
78 |   RecordType(JSTypeRegistry registry, Map<String, RecordProperty> properties) {
79 |     super(registry, null, null);
80 |     setPrettyPrint(true);
81 |

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 595-609
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
603 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
604 |       scopes.push(scope);
605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0038 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 600-639
Documentation:

Method code:
600 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
601 |     String varName = n.getString();
602 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
603 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
604 |     if (value != null) {
605 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
606 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
607 |           getJSType(value));
608 |       return scope;
609 |     } else {
610 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
611 |       if (var != null) {
612 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
613 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
614 | 
615 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
616 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
617 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
618 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
619 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
620 | 
621 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
622 |         // inferred variable.
623 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
624 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
625 |             isInferred &&
626 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
627 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
628 | 
629 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
630 |           type = var.getType();
631 |           if (type == null) {
632 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
633 |           }
634 |         }
635 |       }
636 |     }
637 |     n.setJSType(type);
638 |     return scope;
639 |   }

===== M0052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 341-367
Documentation:

Method code:
341 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
342 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
343 |     // two cases:
344 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
345 |     // and
346 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
347 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
348 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
349 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
350 |     //
351 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
352 |     //
353 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
354 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
355 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
356 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
357 |     //
358 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
359 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
360 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
361 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
362 | 
363 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
364 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
365 |     }
366 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
367 |   }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not

===== M0061 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.processObjectLitProperties(Node,ObjectType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 657-687
Documentation:
/**
     * Process an object literal and all the types on it.
     * @param objLit The OBJECTLIT node.
     * @param objLitType The type of the OBJECTLIT node. This might be a named
     *     type, because of the lends annotation.
     * @param declareOnOwner If true, declare properties on the objLitType as
     *     well. If false, the caller should take care of this.
     */
Method code:
657 |     void processObjectLitProperties(
658 |         Node objLit, ObjectType objLitType,
659 |         boolean declareOnOwner) {
660 |       for (Node keyNode = objLit.getFirstChild(); keyNode != null;
661 |            keyNode = keyNode.getNext()) {
662 |         Node value = keyNode.getFirstChild();
663 |         String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(keyNode);
664 |         JSDocInfo info = keyNode.getJSDocInfo();
665 |         JSType valueType =
666 |             getDeclaredType(keyNode.getSourceFileName(), info, keyNode, value);
667 |         JSType keyType =  objLitType.isEnumType() ?
668 |             objLitType.toMaybeEnumType().getElementsType() :
669 |             NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(keyNode, valueType);
670 | 
671 |         // Try to declare this property in the current scope if it
672 |         // has an authoritative name.
673 |         String qualifiedName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(keyNode);
674 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
675 |           boolean inferred = keyType == null;
676 |           defineSlot(keyNode, objLit, qualifiedName, keyType, inferred);
677 |         } else if (keyType != null) {
678 |           setDeferredType(keyNode, keyType);
679 |         }
680 | 
681 |         if (keyType != null && objLitType != null && declareOnOwner) {
682 |           // Declare this property on its object literal.
683 |           boolean isExtern = keyNode.isFromExterns();
684 |           objLitType.defineDeclaredProperty(memberName, keyType, keyNode);
685 |         }
686 |       }
687 |     }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }
316 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionType.java
Original range: 522-540
Documentation:

Method code:
522 |   @Override
523 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
524 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this); // for circularly defined types.
525 | 
526 |     boolean changed = false;
527 |     ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> resolvedTypes = ImmutableList.builder();
528 |     for (JSType alternate : alternates) {
529 |       JSType newAlternate = alternate.resolve(t, scope);
530 |       changed |= (alternate != newAlternate);
531 |       resolvedTypes.add(alternate);
532 |     }
533 |     if (changed) {
534 |       Collection<JSType> newAlternates = resolvedTypes.build();
535 |       Preconditions.checkState(
536 |           newAlternates.hashCode() == this.hashcode);
537 |       alternates = newAlternates;
538 |     }
539 |     return this;
540 |   }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1078-1091
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
1078 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1079 |     if (resolved) {
1080 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
1081 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
1082 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
1083 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1084 |       }
1085 |       return resolveResult;
1086 |     }
1087 |     resolved = true;
1088 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
1089 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
1090 |     return resolveResult;
1091 |   }

   * Checks if two types are equivalent.

Method code:
408 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType jsType) {
409 |     if (jsType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {

   * Skips all EOLs and all empty lines in the JSDoc. Call this method if you
   * want the JSDoc entry to span multiple lines.

2304 |   private void skipEOLs() {

2308 |         next();
2309 |       }
2310 |     }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFieldType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2136-2164
Documentation:
/**
   * FieldType := FieldName | FieldName ':' TypeExpression
   */
Method code:
2136 |   private Node parseFieldType(JsDocToken token) {
2137 |     Node fieldName = parseFieldName(token);
2138 | 
2139 |     if (fieldName == null) {
2140 |       return null;
2141 |     }
2142 | 
2143 |     skipEOLs();
2144 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
2145 |       return fieldName;
2146 |     }
2147 | 
2148 |     // Move to the colon.
2149 |     next();
2150 | 
2151 |     // Move to the token after the colon and parse
2152 |     // the type expression.
2153 |     skipEOLs();
2154 |     Node typeExpression = parseTypeExpression(next());
2155 | 
2156 |     if (typeExpression == null) {
2157 |       return null;
2158 |     }
2159 | 
2160 |     Node fieldType = newNode(Token.COLON);
2161 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(fieldName);
2162 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(typeExpression);
2163 |     return fieldType;
2164 |   }

     * Defines a VAR initialization.

Method code:
735 |     void defineVar(Node n, Node parent) {
736 |       assertDefinitionNode(n, Token.VAR);
737 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
738 |       if (n.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
739 |         if (info != null) {
740 |           // multiple children
741 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, MULTIPLE_VAR_DEF));
742 |         }
743 |         for (Node name : n.children()) {
744 |           defineName(name, n, parent, name.getJSDocInfo());
745 |         }
746 |       } else {
747 |         Node name = n.getFirstChild();
748 |         defineName(name, n, parent,
749 |             (info != null) ? info : name.getJSDocInfo());
750 |       }
751 |     }

===== M0125 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JoinOp.BinaryJoinOp.apply(List<L>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JoinOp.java
Original range: 36-50
Documentation:

Method code:
36 |     @Override
37 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {
38 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!values.isEmpty());
39 |       int size = values.size();
40 |       if (size == 1) {
41 |         return values.get(0);
42 |       } else if (size == 2) {
43 |         return apply(values.get(0), values.get(1));
44 |       } else {
45 |         int mid = computeMidPoint(size);
46 |         return apply(
47 |             apply(values.subList(0, mid)),
48 |             apply(values.subList(mid, size)));
49 |       }
50 |     }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 324-371
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
324 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
325 |     if (info != null) {
326 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
327 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
328 | 
329 |       // base type
330 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
331 |         if (isConstructor) {
332 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
333 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
334 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
335 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
336 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
337 |           }
338 |         } else {
339 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
340 |         }
341 |       }
342 | 
343 |       // implemented interfaces
344 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
345 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
346 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
347 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
348 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
349 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
350 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
351 |           }
352 |         }
353 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
354 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
355 |       }
356 | 
357 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
358 |       if (isInterface) {
359 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
360 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
361 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
362 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
363 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
364 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
365 |           }
366 |         }
367 |       }
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     return this;
371 |   }

/**
   * FieldTypeList := FieldType | FieldType ',' FieldTypeList
   */
Method code:
2105 |   private Node parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken token) {
2106 |     Node fieldTypeList = newNode(Token.LB);
2107 | 
2108 |     do {
2109 |       Node fieldType = parseFieldType(token);
2110 | 
2111 |       if (fieldType == null) {
2112 |         return null;
2113 |       }
2114 | 
2115 |       fieldTypeList.addChildToBack(fieldType);
2116 | 
2117 |       skipEOLs();
2118 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
2119 |         break;
2120 |       }
2121 | 
2122 |       // Move to the comma token.
2123 |       next();
2124 | 
2125 |       // Move to the token passed the comma.
2126 |       skipEOLs();
2127 |       token = next();
2128 |     } while (true);
2129 | 
2130 |     return fieldTypeList;
2131 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 465-508
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
465 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
466 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
467 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
468 |     switch (left.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         String varName = left.getString();
471 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
472 | 
473 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
474 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
475 |         // This has two purposes:
476 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
477 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
478 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
479 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
480 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
481 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
482 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
483 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
484 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
485 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
486 |         }
487 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
488 |             resultType : null);
489 | 
490 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
491 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
492 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
493 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
494 |         }
495 |         break;
496 |       case Token.GETPROP:
497 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
498 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
499 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
500 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
501 |               resultType);
502 |         }
503 | 
504 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
505 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
506 |         break;
507 |     }
508 |   }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverse(Node)

Original range: 271-282

/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively.
   */
Method code:
271 |   public void traverse(Node root) {

===== M0177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 675-711
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
675 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
676 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
677 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType);
678 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
679 | 
680 |     if (existingType != null) {
681 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
682 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
683 |         FunctionType existingFn =
684 |             isInstanceObject ?
685 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
686 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
687 | 
688 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
689 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
690 |         }
691 | 
692 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
693 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
694 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
695 |         }
696 | 
697 |         return existingFn;
698 |       } else {
699 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
700 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
701 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
702 |       }
703 |     }
704 | 
705 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
706 | 
707 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
708 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
709 |     }
710 |     return fnType;
711 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1998-2037
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
1998 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
1999 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
2000 |     if (alternate != null) {
2001 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
2002 |     }
2003 | 
2004 |     Node expr = null;
2005 |     do {
2006 |       if (expr != null) {
2007 |         skipEOLs();
2008 |         token = next();
2009 |         Preconditions.checkState(
2010 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
2011 | 
2012 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;
2013 |         if (isPipe && match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
2014 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatibility.
2015 |           next();
2016 |         }
2017 |         skipEOLs();
2018 |         token = next();
2019 |       }
2020 |       expr = parseTypeExpression(token);
2021 |       if (expr == null) {
2022 |         return null;
2023 |       }
2024 | 
2025 |       union.addChildToBack(expr);
2026 |       // We support commas for backwards compatibility.
2027 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));
2028 | 
2029 |     if (alternate == null) {
2030 |       skipEOLs();
2031 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2032 |         return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
2033 |       }
2034 |       next();
2035 |     }
2036 |     return union;
2037 |   }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.

   *     normal JsDoc, or no JsDoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 223-245
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
230 |            arg != null;
231 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
232 |         String argName = arg.getString();
233 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);

===== M0259 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.

   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.

===== M0283 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
374 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
375 |     if (prototype == null) {
376 |       return false;
377 |     }
378 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
379 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
384 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
385 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
386 | 
387 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
388 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
389 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
390 | 
391 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
392 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
393 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
394 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
398 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
399 |       if (superClass != null) {
400 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (isInterface()) {
404 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
405 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
406 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
407 |           }
408 |         }
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
413 |       clearCachedValues();
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     return true;
417 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 841-942
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
841 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
842 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
843 |         @Nullable String name,
844 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
845 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
846 | 
847 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
848 | 
849 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
850 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
851 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
852 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
853 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
854 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
855 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
856 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
857 |             functionType = aliasedType;
858 | 
859 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
860 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
861 |             }
862 |           }
863 |         }
864 |       }
865 | 
866 |       if (functionType == null) {
867 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
868 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
869 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
870 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
871 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
872 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
873 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
874 | 
875 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
876 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
877 | 
878 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
879 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
880 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
881 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
882 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
883 |           }
884 |         }
885 | 
886 |         if (functionType == null) {
887 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
888 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
889 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
890 |           Var ownerVar = null;
891 |           String propName = null;
892 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
893 |           if (ownerName != null) {
894 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
895 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
896 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
897 |             }
898 |             if (name != null) {
899 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
900 |             }
901 |           }
902 | 
903 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
904 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
905 |             overriddenPropType =
906 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
907 |           }
908 | 
909 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
910 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
911 |                   scope)
912 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
913 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
914 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
915 |               .inferReturnType(info)
916 |               .inferInheritance(info);
917 | 
918 | 
919 |           // Infer the context type.
920 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
921 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
922 |             builder.inferThisType(
923 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
924 |             searchedForThisType = true;
925 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
926 |             builder.inferThisType(info, ownerNode.getJSType());
927 |             searchedForThisType = true;
928 |           }
929 | 
930 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
931 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
932 |           }
933 | 
934 |           functionType = builder
935 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
936 |               .buildAndRegister();
937 |         }
938 |       }
939 | 
940 |       // all done
941 |       return functionType;
942 |     }

===== M0321 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(AbstractCompiler,List<Node>,Callback)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 461-465

/**
   * Traverses a list of node trees.
   */
Method code:
461 |   public static void traverseRoots(

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1259-1307

/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1259 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1260 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1261 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1262 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1263 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1264 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1265 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1266 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1267 |       } else if (info != null) {
1268 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1269 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1270 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1271 |           } else {
1272 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1273 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1274 |           }
1275 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1276 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1277 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1278 |         } else {
1279 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1280 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1281 |             JSType knownType = null;
1282 |             if (rValue != null) {
1283 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1284 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1285 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1286 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1287 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1288 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1289 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1290 |                 // reasons.
1291 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1292 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1293 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1294 |                     && lValue.isName()
1295 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1296 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1297 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1298 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1299 |                 }
1300 |               }
1301 |             }
1302 |           }
1303 |         }
1304 |       }
1305 | 
1306 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1307 |     }

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 243-301
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
243 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
244 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
245 |     if (oldType == null) {
246 |       return this;
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
250 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
251 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
252 |       // Not a function literal.
253 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
254 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
255 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
256 |       }
257 |     } else {
258 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
259 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
260 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
261 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
262 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
263 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
264 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
265 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
266 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
267 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
268 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
269 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
270 | 
271 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
272 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
273 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
274 | 
275 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
276 |           // arguments.
277 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
278 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
279 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
280 |           }
281 |         } else {
282 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
283 |               paramBuilder,
284 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
285 |               warnedAboutArgList,
286 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
287 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
288 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
289 |         }
290 |       }
291 | 
292 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
293 |       // but make them optional.
294 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
295 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
299 |     }
300 |     return this;
301 |   }

3034 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1683-1724
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1683 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1684 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1685 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1686 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1687 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1688 |       // {?} - right curly
1689 |       // {?=} - equals
1690 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1691 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1692 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1693 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1694 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1695 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1696 |       token = next();
1697 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1698 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1699 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1700 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1701 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1702 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1703 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1704 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1705 |       }
1706 | 
1707 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1708 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1709 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1710 |     } else {
1711 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1712 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1713 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1714 |           next();
1715 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1716 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1717 |           next();
1718 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1719 |         }
1720 |       }
1721 | 
1722 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1723 |     }
1724 |   }

===== M0359 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-197
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
184 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
185 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
186 |         } else {
187 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
188 |         }
189 |       case Token.SWITCH:
190 |       case Token.CASE:
191 |       case Token.CATCH:
192 |       case Token.WITH:
193 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |       default:
195 |         return false;
196 |     }
197 |   }

1133 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);

1135 |     }
1136 |     if (changed) {
