===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1614-1665
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a RecordType from the nodes representing said record type.
   * @param n The node with type info.
   * @param sourceName The source file name.
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name lookups.
   */
Method code:
1614 |   private JSType createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node n, String sourceName,
1615 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1616 | 
1617 |     RecordTypeBuilder builder = new RecordTypeBuilder(this);
1618 | 
1619 |     // For each of the fields in the record type.
1620 |     for (Node fieldTypeNode = n.getFirstChild();
1621 |          fieldTypeNode != null;
1622 |          fieldTypeNode = fieldTypeNode.getNext()) {
1623 | 
1624 |       // Get the property's name.
1625 |       Node fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode;
1626 |       boolean hasType = false;
1627 | 
1628 |       if (fieldTypeNode.getType() == Token.COLON) {
1629 |         fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode.getFirstChild();
1630 |         hasType = true;
1631 |       }
1632 | 
1633 |       String fieldName = fieldNameNode.getString();
1634 | 
1635 |       // TODO(user): Move this into the lexer/parser.
1636 |       // Remove the string literal characters around a field name,
1637 |       // if any.
1638 |       if (fieldName.startsWith("'") || fieldName.startsWith("\"")) {
1639 |         fieldName = fieldName.substring(1, fieldName.length() - 1);
1640 |       }
1641 | 
1642 |       // Get the property's type.
1643 |       JSType fieldType = null;
1644 | 
1645 |       if (hasType) {
1646 |         // We have a declared type.
1647 |         fieldType = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1648 |             fieldTypeNode.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1649 |       } else {
1650 |         // Otherwise, the type is UNKNOWN.
1651 |         fieldType = getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1652 |       }
1653 | 
1654 |       // Add the property to the record.
1655 |       if (builder.addProperty(fieldName, fieldType, fieldNameNode) == null) {
1656 |         // Duplicate field name, warning and skip
1657 |         reporter.warning(
1658 |             "Duplicate record field " + fieldName,
1659 |             sourceName,
1660 |             n.getLineno(), fieldNameNode.getCharno());
1661 |       }
1662 |     }
1663 | 
1664 |     return builder.build();
1665 |   }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1905-1907
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1905 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1906 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1907 |   }

===== M0423 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 557-587
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
557 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
558 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
559 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
560 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
561 |     if (isOptional) {
562 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
563 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
564 |       // error can be reported.
565 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
566 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
567 |         emittedWarning = true;
568 |       }
569 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
570 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
571 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
572 |         emittedWarning = true;
573 |       }
574 |     } else {
575 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
576 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
577 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
578 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
579 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
580 |         } else {
581 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
582 |         }
583 |         emittedWarning = true;
584 |       }
585 |     }
586 |     return emittedWarning;
587 |   }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0459 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1087-1226
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1087 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1088 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1089 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1090 | 
1091 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1092 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1093 |           isGlobalVar &&
1094 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1095 |            parent.isFunction());
1096 | 
1097 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1098 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1099 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1100 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1101 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1102 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1103 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1104 | 
1105 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1106 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1107 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1108 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1109 |         }
1110 |       }
1111 | 
1112 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1113 |       // the extern info from the node.
1114 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1115 |       Var newVar = null;
1116 | 
1117 |       // declared in closest scope?
1118 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1119 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1120 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1121 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1122 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1123 |       } else {
1124 |         if (!inferred) {
1125 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1126 |         }
1127 | 
1128 |         newVar =
1129 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1130 | 
1131 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1132 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1133 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1134 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1135 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1136 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1137 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1138 |           }
1139 |         }
1140 |       }
1141 | 
1142 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1143 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1144 |       if (fnType != null &&
1145 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1146 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1147 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1148 |             !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1149 |           // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1150 |           FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1151 |           ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1152 | 
1153 |           // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1154 |           // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1155 |           // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1156 |           // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1157 |           // the same node.
1158 |           //
1159 |           // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1160 |           // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1161 |           prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1162 | 
1163 |           String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1164 | 
1165 |           // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1166 |           // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1167 |           // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1168 |           Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1169 |           if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1170 |             scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1171 |           }
1172 | 
1173 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1174 |               n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1175 |               /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1176 |               superClassCtor == null ||
1177 |               superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1178 |                   getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1179 | 
1180 |           // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1181 |           // it constructs itself.
1182 |           if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1183 |               !isExtern &&
1184 |               // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1185 |               // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1186 |               // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1187 |               // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1188 |               // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1189 |               // this.
1190 |               variableName.equals(
1191 |                   fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1192 |             compiler.report(
1193 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1194 |                     fnType.isConstructor() ?
1195 |                     CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1196 |                     variableName));
1197 |           }
1198 |         }
1199 |       }
1200 | 
1201 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1202 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1203 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1204 |         if (inferred) {
1205 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1206 |               type == null ?
1207 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1208 |               type,
1209 |               n);
1210 |         } else {
1211 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1212 |         }
1213 |       }
1214 | 
1215 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1216 |           && type != null
1217 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1218 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1219 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1220 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1221 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1222 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1223 |         globalThisCtor
1224 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1225 |       }
1226 |     }

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 592-654
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
592 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
593 |     if (returnType == null) {
594 |       // Infer return types.
595 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
596 |       // competing needs.
597 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
598 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
599 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
600 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
601 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
602 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
603 |       // nothing.
604 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
605 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
606 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
607 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
608 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
609 |       }
610 |     }
611 | 
612 |     if (returnType == null) {
613 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
617 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
618 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     FunctionType fnType;
622 |     if (isConstructor) {
623 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
624 |     } else if (isInterface) {
625 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
626 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
627 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
628 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
629 |       }
630 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
631 |     } else {
632 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
633 |           .withName(fnName)
634 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
635 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
636 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
637 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
638 |           .withTemplateName(templateTypeName)
639 |           .build();
640 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
644 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
648 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeName();
652 | 
653 |     return fnType;
654 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0475 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1569-1604
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1569 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1570 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1571 | 
1572 |     skipEOLs();
1573 | 
1574 |     boolean restArg = false;
1575 |     token = next();
1576 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1577 |       token = next();
1578 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1579 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1580 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1581 |       }
1582 |       restArg = true;
1583 |     }
1584 | 
1585 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1586 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1587 |       skipEOLs();
1588 |       if (restArg) {
1589 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1590 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1591 |         next();
1592 |         skipEOLs();
1593 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1594 |       }
1595 | 
1596 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1597 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1598 |       } else {
1599 |         next();
1600 |       }
1601 |     }
1602 | 
1603 |     return typeNode;
1604 |   }

===== M0482 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 251-414
Documentation:

Method code:
251 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
252 |     switch (n.getType()) {
253 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
254 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
255 |         break;
256 | 
257 |       case Token.NAME:
258 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
259 |         break;
260 | 
261 |       case Token.GETPROP:
262 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
263 |         break;
264 | 
265 |       case Token.AND:
266 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
267 |             .createChildFlowScope();
268 |         break;
269 | 
270 |       case Token.OR:
271 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
272 |             .createChildFlowScope();
273 |         break;
274 | 
275 |       case Token.HOOK:
276 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
277 |         break;
278 | 
279 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
280 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
281 |         break;
282 | 
283 |       case Token.CALL:
284 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.NEW:
288 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
289 |         break;
290 | 
291 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
292 |       case Token.ADD:
293 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.POS:
297 |       case Token.NEG:
298 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
299 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
300 |         break;
301 | 
302 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
303 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
304 |         break;
305 | 
306 |       case Token.THIS:
307 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
308 |         break;
309 | 
310 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
311 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
312 |       case Token.LSH:
313 |       case Token.RSH:
314 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
315 |       case Token.URSH:
316 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
317 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
318 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
319 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
320 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
321 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
322 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
323 |       case Token.DIV:
324 |       case Token.MOD:
325 |       case Token.BITAND:
326 |       case Token.BITXOR:
327 |       case Token.BITOR:
328 |       case Token.MUL:
329 |       case Token.SUB:
330 |       case Token.DEC:
331 |       case Token.INC:
332 |       case Token.BITNOT:
333 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
334 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
335 |         break;
336 | 
337 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
338 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
339 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
340 |         break;
341 | 
342 |       case Token.COMMA:
343 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
344 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
348 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
349 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.DELPROP:
353 |       case Token.LT:
354 |       case Token.LE:
355 |       case Token.GT:
356 |       case Token.GE:
357 |       case Token.NOT:
358 |       case Token.EQ:
359 |       case Token.NE:
360 |       case Token.SHEQ:
361 |       case Token.SHNE:
362 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
363 |       case Token.IN:
364 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
365 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
366 |         break;
367 | 
368 |       case Token.GETELEM:
369 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
370 |         break;
371 | 
372 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
373 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
374 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
375 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
376 |         }
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       case Token.SWITCH:
380 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
381 |         break;
382 | 
383 |       case Token.RETURN:
384 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
385 |         break;
386 | 
387 |       case Token.VAR:
388 |       case Token.THROW:
389 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
390 |         break;
391 | 
392 |       case Token.CATCH:
393 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
394 |         break;
395 |     }
396 |     if (!n.isFunction()) {
397 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
398 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
399 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
400 | 
401 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
402 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
403 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
404 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
405 |             n.getParent().isExprResult()) {
406 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
407 |         }
408 | 
409 |         n.setJSType(castType);
410 |       }
411 |     }
412 | 
413 |     return scope;
414 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getLineno()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 226-226

===== M0499 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1567-1581
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1567 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1568 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1569 |       return getString();
1570 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1571 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1572 |       if (left == null) {
1573 |         return null;
1574 |       }
1575 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1576 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1577 |       return "this";
1578 |     } else {
1579 |       return null;
1580 |     }
1581 |   }

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

/**
   * Looks up a type by name. To allow for forward references to types, an
   * unrecognized string has to be bound to a NamedType object that will be
   * resolved later.
   *
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name resolution.
   * @param jsTypeName The name string.
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file where this reference appears.
   * @param lineno The line number of the reference.
   * @return a NamedType if the string argument is not one of the known types,
   *     otherwise the corresponding JSType object.
   */
Method code:
898 |   public JSType getType(StaticScope<JSType> scope, String jsTypeName,
899 |       String sourceName, int lineno, int charno) {
900 |     JSType type = getType(jsTypeName);
901 |     if (type == null) {
902 |       // TODO(user): Each instance should support named type creation using
903 |       // interning.
904 |       NamedType namedType =
905 |           new NamedType(this, jsTypeName, sourceName, lineno, charno);
906 |       unresolvedNamedTypes.put(scope, namedType);
907 |       type = namedType;
908 |     }
909 |     return type;
910 |   }

===== M0530 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1730-1756
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1730 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1731 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1732 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1733 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1734 |       skipEOLs();
1735 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1736 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1737 |       skipEOLs();
1738 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1739 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1740 |       skipEOLs();
1741 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1742 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1743 |       String string = stream.getString();
1744 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1745 |         skipEOLs();
1746 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1747 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1748 |         return newStringNode(string);
1749 |       } else {
1750 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1751 |       }
1752 |     }
1753 | 
1754 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1755 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1756 |   }

85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());

87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);

===== M0548 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 191-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
191 |   @Override
192 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
193 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
194 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
195 |     // show up in the type registry.
196 |     Scope newScope = null;
197 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
198 |     if (parent == null) {
199 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
200 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
201 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
202 | 
203 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
204 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
205 | 
206 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
207 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
208 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
209 |     } else {
210 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
211 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
212 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
213 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
218 | 
219 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
220 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
221 |     // build properties on.
222 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
223 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
224 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
225 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
226 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
227 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
229 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
230 |         }
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     if (parent == null) {
235 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
236 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
237 |           delegateCallingConventions);
238 |     }
239 |     return newScope;
240 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java

Method code:
804 |     @Override
805 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
806 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
807 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
808 |       }
809 | 
810 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
811 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
812 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
813 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
814 |             reportGetter(el);
815 |             continue;
816 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
817 |             reportSetter(el);
818 |             continue;
819 |           }
820 |         }
821 | 
822 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
823 |         key.setType(Token.STRING_KEY);
824 | 
825 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
826 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
827 |           key.setType(Token.GETTER_DEF);
828 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
829 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
830 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
831 |           }
832 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
833 |           key.setType(Token.SETTER_DEF);
834 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
835 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
836 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
837 |           }
838 |         }
839 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
840 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
841 |       }
842 |       return node;
843 |     }

===== M0560 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.

842 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
843 | 
844 |                   if (type == null) {
845 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
846 |                     // recovering parsing
847 |                   } else {
848 |                     switch (annotation) {
849 |                       case DEFINE:
850 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
851 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
852 |                               lineno, charno);
853 |                         }
854 |                         break;
855 | 
856 |                       case RETURN:
857 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
858 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
859 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
860 |                           break;
861 |                         }
862 | 
863 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
864 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
865 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
866 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
867 | 
868 |                           String returnDescription =
869 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
870 | 
871 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
872 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
873 |                                 returnDescription);
874 |                           }
875 | 
876 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
877 |                         } else {
878 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
879 |                         }
880 |                         continue retry;
881 | 
882 |                       case THIS:
883 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
884 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
885 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
886 |                         }
887 |                         break;
888 | 
889 |                       case TYPE:
890 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
891 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
892 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
893 |                         }
894 |                         break;
895 | 
896 |                       case TYPEDEF:
897 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
898 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
899 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
900 |                         }
901 |                         break;
902 |                     }
903 |                   }
904 | 
905 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
906 |                   continue retry;
907 |               }
908 |             }
909 |           }
910 |           break;
911 | 
912 |         case EOC:
913 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
914 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
915 |           }
916 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
917 |           return true;
918 | 
919 |         case EOF:
920 |           // discard any accumulated information
921 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
922 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
923 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
924 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
925 |           return false;
926 | 
927 |         case EOL:
928 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
929 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
930 |           }
931 |           token = next();
932 |           continue retry;
933 | 
934 |         default:
935 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
936 |             token = next();
937 |             continue retry;
938 |           } else {
939 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
940 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
941 |             continue retry;
942 |           }
943 |       }
944 | 
945 |       // next token
946 |       token = next();
947 |     }
948 |   }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

393 |     protected void initialize() {

===== M0577 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0610 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3005-3031
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3005 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3006 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3007 |     if (info == null) {
3008 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3009 |       if (parent == null) {
3010 |         return null;
3011 |       }
3012 | 
3013 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3014 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3015 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3024 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3025 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3026 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3027 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3028 |       }
3029 |     }
3030 |     return info;
3031 |   }

===== M0611 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0615 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0618 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1763-1798
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1763 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1764 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1765 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1766 |     }
1767 | 
1768 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1769 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1770 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1771 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1772 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1773 |       skipEOLs();
1774 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1775 |         next();
1776 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1777 |       }
1778 |     }
1779 | 
1780 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1781 | 
1782 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1783 |       next();
1784 |       skipEOLs();
1785 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1786 |       if (memberType != null) {
1787 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1788 | 
1789 |         skipEOLs();
1790 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1791 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1792 |         }
1793 | 
1794 |         next();
1795 |       }
1796 |     }
1797 |     return typeNameNode;
1798 |   }

===== M0637 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1654-1684
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1654 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1655 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1656 |         Node n = stub.node;
1657 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1658 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1659 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1660 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1661 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1662 | 
1663 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1664 |           continue;
1665 |         }
1666 | 
1667 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1668 |         // in the type registry.
1669 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1670 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1671 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1672 | 
1673 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1674 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1675 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1676 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1677 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1678 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1679 |         } else {
1680 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1681 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1682 |         }
1683 |       }
1684 |     }

Original range: 910-931
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |         return true;
922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }
925 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
926 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
927 |         return true;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 |     return false;
931 |   }

===== M0648 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0650 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 498-555
Documentation:

Method code:
498 |     @Override
499 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
501 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
502 | 
503 |       switch (n.getType()) {
504 |         case Token.CALL:
505 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
506 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
507 |           break;
508 | 
509 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
510 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
511 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
512 |           }
513 | 
514 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
515 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
516 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 | 
520 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
521 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
522 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
523 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
524 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
525 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
526 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
527 |           }
528 |           break;
529 | 
530 |         case Token.CATCH:
531 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.VAR:
535 |           defineVar(n, parent);
536 |           break;
537 | 
538 |         case Token.GETPROP:
539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
541 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
542 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 |       }
546 | 
547 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
549 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
550 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
551 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
552 |         }
553 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
554 |       }
555 |     }

===== M0653 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken)

Original range: 1112-1126
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1112 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1113 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1114 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1115 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1116 | 
1117 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1118 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1119 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1120 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1121 | 
1122 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1123 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1124 |     }
1125 |     return typeNode;
1126 |   }

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0661 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0681 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 466-496
Documentation:

Method code:
466 |     @Override
467 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
468 |         Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
470 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
471 |           n.isScript()) {
472 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
473 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
477 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
478 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
480 | 
481 |       if (descend) {
482 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
483 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
484 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
485 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
486 |                child != null;
487 |                child = child.getNext()) {
488 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
489 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
490 |             }
491 |           }
492 |         }
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       return descend;
496 |     }

===== M0699 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2024-2036
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2024 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2025 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2026 |         n.isGetElem());
2027 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2028 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2029 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2030 |         || parent.isVar()
2031 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2032 |         || parent.isDec()
2033 |         || parent.isInc()
2034 |         || parent.isParamList()
2035 |         || parent.isCatch();
2036 |   }

===== M0708 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1960-2000
Documentation:

Method code:
1960 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1961 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
1962 |         return;
1963 |       }
1964 | 
1965 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
1966 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
1967 |       }
1968 | 
1969 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 2) {
1970 |         // We only need to worry about escaped variables at depth 3.
1971 |         // An variable escaped at depth 2 is, by definition, a global variable.
1972 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
1973 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
1974 |         return;
1975 |       }
1976 | 
1977 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
1978 |         String name = n.getString();
1979 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
1980 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
1981 |         if (var != null) {
1982 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
1983 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
1984 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
1985 |           }
1986 |         }
1987 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
1988 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
1989 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
1990 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
1991 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
1992 |         if (var != null) {
1993 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
1994 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
1995 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
1996 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
1997 |           }
1998 |         }
1999 |       }
2000 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.toStringHelper(boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 352-396
Documentation:

Method code:
352 |   @Override
353 |   String toStringHelper(boolean forAnnotations) {
354 |     if (hasReferenceName()) {
355 |       return getReferenceName();
356 |     } else if (prettyPrint) {
357 |       // Don't pretty print recursively.
358 |       prettyPrint = false;
359 | 
360 |       // Use a tree set so that the properties are sorted.
361 |       Set<String> propertyNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
362 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
363 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
364 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;
365 |            current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
366 |         propertyNames.addAll(current.getOwnPropertyNames());
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
370 |       sb.append("{");
371 | 
372 |       int i = 0;
373 |       for (String property : propertyNames) {
374 |         if (i > 0) {
375 |           sb.append(", ");
376 |         }
377 | 
378 |         sb.append(property);
379 |         sb.append(": ");
380 |         sb.append(getPropertyType(property).toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
381 | 
382 |         ++i;
383 |         if (!forAnnotations && i == MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES) {
384 |           sb.append(", ...");
385 |           break;
386 |         }
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       sb.append("}");
390 | 
391 |       prettyPrint = true;
392 |       return sb.toString();
393 |     } else {
394 |       return forAnnotations ? "?" : "{...}";
395 |     }
396 |   }

===== M0720 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 819-843
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
819 |   @Override
820 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
821 |     FunctionType that =
822 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
823 |     if (that == null) {
824 |       return false;
825 |     }
826 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
827 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
828 |         return this == that;
829 |       }
830 |       return false;
831 |     }
832 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
833 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
834 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
835 |       }
836 |       return false;
837 |     }
838 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
839 |       return false;
840 |     }
841 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
842 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
843 |   }

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {

212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;

===== M0733 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 426-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
426 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
427 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
428 |     if (argsParent == null) {
429 |       if (info == null) {
430 |         return this;
431 |       } else {
432 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
433 |       }
434 |     }
435 | 
436 |     // arguments
437 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
438 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
439 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
443 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
444 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
445 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
446 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
447 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
448 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
449 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
450 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
451 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
452 | 
453 |       // type from JSDocInfo
454 |       JSType parameterType = null;
455 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
456 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
457 | 
458 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
459 |         parameterType =
460 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
461 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
462 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
463 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
464 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
465 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
466 |       } else {
467 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
468 |       }
469 | 
470 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
471 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
472 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
473 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
474 |         }
475 |         foundTemplateType = true;
476 |       }
477 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
478 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
479 |           isOptionalParam,
480 |           isVarArgs);
481 | 
482 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
483 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
484 |       }
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
488 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
489 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
490 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
491 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
492 |       }
493 |     }
494 | 
495 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
496 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
497 |     }
498 | 
499 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
500 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
504 |     return this;
505 |   }
