/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1452 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1453 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1455 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1456 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1457 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1458 | 
1459 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1460 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1461 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1462 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1463 | 
1464 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1465 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1466 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1467 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1468 |         }
1469 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1470 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1471 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1472 | 
1473 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1474 |         return createOptionalType(
1475 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1476 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1477 | 
1478 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1479 |         return createOptionalType(
1480 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1481 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1482 | 
1483 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1484 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1485 | 
1486 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1487 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1488 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1489 | 
1490 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1491 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1492 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1493 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1494 |           builder.addAlternate(
1495 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1496 |         }
1497 |         return builder.build();
1498 | 
1499 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1500 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1501 | 
1502 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1503 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1504 | 
1505 |       case Token.STRING:
1506 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1507 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1508 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1509 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1510 |         }
1511 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1512 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1513 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1514 |           if (typeList != null &&
1515 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1516 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1517 |             JSType parameterType =
1518 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1519 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1520 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1521 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1522 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1523 |               JSType indexType =
1524 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1525 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1527 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1528 |             }
1529 |           }
1530 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1531 |         } else {
1532 |           return namedType;
1533 |         }
1534 | 
1535 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1536 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1537 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1538 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1539 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1540 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1541 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1542 |           thisType =
1543 |               ObjectType.cast(
1544 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1545 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1546 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1547 |           if (thisType == null) {
1548 |             reporter.warning(
1549 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1550 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1551 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1552 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1553 |                 sourceName,
1554 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1555 |           }
1556 | 
1557 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1558 |           current = current.getNext();
1559 |         }
1560 | 
1561 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1562 | 
1563 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1564 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1565 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1566 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1567 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1568 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1569 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1570 |               } else {
1571 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1572 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1573 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1574 |               }
1575 |             } else {
1576 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1577 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1578 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1579 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1580 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1581 |                   reporter.warning(
1582 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1583 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1584 |                 }
1585 |               } else {
1586 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1587 |               }
1588 |             }
1589 |           }
1590 |           current = current.getNext();
1591 |         }
1592 | 
1593 |         JSType returnType =
1594 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1595 | 
1596 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1597 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1598 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1599 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1600 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1601 |             .build();
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1605 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1606 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 648-707
Documentation:

Method code:
648 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
649 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
650 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(type);
651 | 
652 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null; name = name.getNext()) {
653 |       scope = traverse(name.getFirstChild(), scope);
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types, or changed with
657 |     // type casts.
658 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
659 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
660 |     // TODO(nicksantos): There should be an "anonymous object" type that
661 |     // we can check for here.
662 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
663 |     if (objectType == null) {
664 |       return scope;
665 |     }
666 | 
667 |     boolean hasLendsName = n.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
668 |         n.getJSDocInfo().getLendsName() != null;
669 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName() && !hasLendsName) {
670 |       return scope;
671 |     }
672 | 
673 |     String qObjName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(
674 |         NodeUtil.getBestLValue(n));
675 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
676 |          name = name.getNext()) {
677 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
678 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(name);
679 |       if (memberName != null) {
680 |         JSType rawValueType =  name.getFirstChild().getJSType();
681 |         JSType valueType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
682 |             name, rawValueType);
683 |         if (valueType == null) {
684 |           valueType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
685 |         }
686 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, valueType, name);
687 | 
688 |         // Do normal flow inference if this is a direct property assignment.
689 |         if (qObjName != null && name.isStringKey()) {
690 |           String qKeyName = qObjName + "." + memberName;
691 |           Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(qKeyName);
692 |           JSType oldType = var == null ? null : var.getType();
693 |           if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
694 |             var.setType(oldType == null ?
695 |                 valueType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(oldType));
696 |           }
697 | 
698 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(name, qKeyName,
699 |               oldType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : oldType,
700 |               valueType);
701 |         }
702 |       } else {
703 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
704 |       }
705 |     }
706 |     return scope;
707 |   }

===== M0753 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0757 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 137-249
Documentation:

Method code:
137 |   @Override
138 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
139 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
140 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
141 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
142 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
143 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
144 |     // worthwhile.
145 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
146 |     Node condition = null;
147 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
148 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
149 | 
150 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
151 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
152 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
153 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
154 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
155 | 
156 |       switch (branch) {
157 |         case ON_TRUE:
158 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
159 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
160 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
161 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
162 | 
163 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
164 | 
165 |             if (item.isVar()) {
166 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
167 |             }
168 |             if (item.isName()) {
169 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
170 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
171 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
172 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
173 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
174 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
175 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
176 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
177 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
178 |                 }
179 |               }
180 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
181 |             }
182 |             newScope = informed;
183 |             break;
184 |           }
185 | 
186 |           // FALL THROUGH
187 | 
188 |         case ON_FALSE:
189 |           if (condition == null) {
190 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
191 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
192 |               condition = source;
193 | 
194 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
195 |               // of the loop.
196 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
197 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
198 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
199 |               }
200 |             }
201 |           }
202 | 
203 |           if (condition != null) {
204 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
205 |                 condition.isOr()) {
206 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
207 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
208 |               // scope on false.
209 |               //
210 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
211 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
212 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
213 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
214 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
215 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
216 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
217 | 
218 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
219 |               // of the loop.
220 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
221 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
222 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
223 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
224 |               }
225 |               newScope =
226 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
227 |                       condition,
228 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
229 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
230 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
231 |             } else {
232 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
233 |               // of the loop.
234 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
235 |                 conditionFlowScope =
236 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
237 |               }
238 |               newScope =
239 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
240 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
241 |             }
242 |           }
243 |           break;
244 |       }
245 | 
246 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
247 |     }
248 |     return result;
249 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0769 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0770 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

   * Create the outermost scope. This scope contains native binding such as

===== M0798 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseNew(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1035-1063
Documentation:

Method code:
1035 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1036 | 
1037 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
1038 |     scope = traverse(constructor, scope);
1039 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
1040 |     JSType type = null;
1041 |     if (constructorType != null) {
1042 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1043 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
1044 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1045 |       } else {
1046 |         FunctionType ct = constructorType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1047 |         if (ct == null && constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {
1048 |           // If constructorType is a NoObjectType, then toMaybeFunctionType will
1049 |           // return null. But NoObjectType implements the FunctionType
1050 |           // interface, precisely because it can validly construct objects.
1051 |           ct = (FunctionType) constructorType;
1052 |         }
1053 |         if (ct != null && ct.isConstructor()) {
1054 |           type = ct.getInstanceType();
1055 |         }
1056 |       }
1057 |     }
1058 |     n.setJSType(type);
1059 |     for (Node arg = constructor.getNext(); arg != null; arg = arg.getNext()) {
1060 |       scope = traverse(arg, scope);
1061 |     }
1062 |     return scope;
1063 |   }

/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */
Method code:
2235 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
2236 |     do {
2237 |       if (token == JsDocToken.EOL || token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
2238 |           token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
2239 |         state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
2240 |         return token;
2241 |       }
2242 |       token = next();
2243 |     } while (true);
2244 |   }

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }
